```python
# --- raffinage reasoning -> rubrique projet (mots-clés déterministes, offline) ---
# ordre = priorité : le 1er motif trouvé gagne. Chemins = feuilles du squelette (Task 2).
_PROJECT_KEYWORDS = [
    ("projets/atelier3d/pricing-stripe", ("pricing", "stripe", "abonnement", "paiement")),
    ("projets/atelier3d/modelisation/3d", ("three.js", "3d", "orbit", "camera", "bbox", "modelisation")),
    ("projets/atelier3d/qa-harness", ("qa-harness", "qa harness", "playwright", "e2e")),
    ("projets/atelier3d/rbac-app", ("rbac", "permission", "role app")),
    ("projets/atelier3d/marque-landing", ("landing", "marque", "branding")),
    ("projets/anamnese/fine-tuning-lora", ("lora", "fine-tuning", "fine tuning", "dataset")),
    ("projets/anamnese/daemon-reve-consolidation", ("daemon", "rêve", "reve", "consolidation", "dream")),
    ("projets/anamnese/moteur-local", ("ollama", "qwen", "moteur local", "embeddings")),
    ("projets/anamnese/bugs-fixes", ("bug", "fix", "crash", "traceback")),
    ("projets/unification/simulations-genesis", ("genesis", "simulation")),
    ("projets/unification/charge-topologique", ("charge topologique", "topologie")),
    ("projets/unification/cosmogonie-vision", ("cosmogonie", "unification")),
    ("projets/atelier3d", ("atelier3d",)),   # fallback projet
    ("projets/anamnese", ("anamnèse", "anamnese")),
]

def refine_reasoning_project(content):
    low = (content or "").lower()
    for path, kws in _PROJECT_KEYWORDS:
        if any(k in low for k in kws):
            return path
    return None

def route_with_refine(role, content):
    path, conf = deterministic_route(role, content)
    if role == "reasoning":
        refined = refine_reasoning_project(content)
        if refined is not None:
            return (refined, 0.85)
    return (path, conf)
```

## Task 5 — Routage LLM Qwen 9B pour l'ambigu (semantic/episodic) + fallback offline

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/migrate/route_llm.py`
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_route_llm.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `engine.Engine(...).chat(messages, stream=False, think=False)`, `engine.Engine().health()->bool`, `migrate.skeleton.SKELETON` (liste des chemins valides).
- Produces :
  - `migrate.route_llm.build_prompt(content: str, valid_paths: list[str]) -> list[dict]` (messages pour `Engine.chat`).
  - `migrate.route_llm.parse_llm_reply(text: str, valid_paths: set[str]) -> tuple[str|None,float]` = extrait un JSON `{"rubric_path":..., "conf":...}` robuste ; renvoie `(None,0.0)` si chemin hors `valid_paths` ou parse KO.
  - `migrate.route_llm.route_ambiguous(content, valid_paths, engine=None) -> tuple[str,float]` = interroge Qwen ; si `engine` None l'instancie ; si `health()` KO ou parse KO → fallback déterministe `("divers", 0.3)` (jamais de crash, offline-safe).

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire `tests/test_route_llm.py` (aucun appel réseau : on **mocke** l'engine) :
```python
from migrate.route_llm import build_prompt, parse_llm_reply, route_ambiguous

VALID = {"personnel-ahmed/materiel", "technique-transverse/sqlite", "divers"}

def test_build_prompt_lists_valid_paths():
    msgs = build_prompt("MacBook Air M4 16 Go", ["personnel-ahmed/materiel", "divers"])
    assert msgs[-1]["role"] == "user"
    assert "personnel-ahmed/materiel" in msgs[-1]["content"]

def test_parse_valid_json():
    p, c = parse_llm_reply('{"rubric_path": "technique-transverse/sqlite", "conf": 0.8}', VALID)
    assert p == "technique-transverse/sqlite" and abs(c - 0.8) < 1e-9

def test_parse_rejects_unknown_path():
    p, c = parse_llm_reply('{"rubric_path": "inexistant/xyz", "conf": 0.9}', VALID)
    assert p is None and c == 0.0

def test_parse_garbage_returns_none():
    assert parse_llm_reply("je pense que c'est du matériel", VALID) == (None, 0.0)

class FakeEngine:
    def __init__(self, reply, ok=True): self._reply, self._ok = reply, ok
    def health(self): return self._ok
    def chat(self, messages, stream=True, think=False): return self._reply

def test_route_ambiguous_uses_engine():
    eng = FakeEngine('{"rubric_path":"technique-transverse/sqlite","conf":0.77}')
    p, c = route_ambiguous("PRAGMA WAL sqlite", VALID, engine=eng)
    assert p == "technique-transverse/sqlite" and abs(c - 0.77) < 1e-9

def test_route_ambiguous_fallback_when_engine_down():
    eng = FakeEngine("", ok=False)
    p, c = route_ambiguous("n'importe quoi", VALID, engine=eng)
    assert p == "divers" and c == 0.3

    eng = FakeEngine("bla bla non-JSON")
    p, c = route_ambiguous("truc", VALID, engine=eng)
    assert p == "divers" and c == 0.3
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `migrate/route_llm.py` :
```python
"""Routage LLM local (Qwen 9B via engine.py/Ollama) pour l'ambigu (semantic/episodic).
100% offline (modèle local). Fallback dur 'divers'/0.3 si Ollama down ou réponse non parsable
-> la migration ne crashe jamais et reste offline-safe."""
import re, json

FALLBACK = ("divers", 0.3)

def build_prompt(content, valid_paths):
    listing = "\n".join(f"- {p}" for p in valid_paths)
    sys = ("Tu ranges un souvenir dans UNE rubrique d'un arbre de connaissances. "
           "Réponds UNIQUEMENT par un objet JSON compact "
           '{"rubric_path": "<un chemin EXACT de la liste>", "conf": <0..1>}. '
           "Aucun autre texte.")
    usr = (f"Rubriques valides:\n{listing}\n\n"
           f"Souvenir:\n{content[:1500]}\n\n"
           "JSON:")
    return [{"role": "system", "content": sys}, {"role": "user", "content": usr}]

def parse_llm_reply(text, valid_paths):
    if not text:
        return (None, 0.0)
    m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.S)
    if not m:
        return (None, 0.0)
    try:
        obj = json.loads(m.group(0))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return (None, 0.0)
    path = obj.get("rubric_path")
    if path not in valid_paths:
        return (None, 0.0)
    try:
        conf = float(obj.get("conf", 0.5))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        conf = 0.5
    return (path, max(0.0, min(1.0, conf)))

def route_ambiguous(content, valid_paths, engine=None):
    valid = set(valid_paths)
    if engine is None:
        from engine import Engine
        engine = Engine()
    try:
        if not engine.health():
            return FALLBACK
        reply = engine.chat(build_prompt(content, list(valid)), stream=False, think=False)
    except Exception:
        return FALLBACK
    path, conf = parse_llm_reply(reply, valid)
    if path is None:
        return FALLBACK
    return (path, conf)
```

## Task 6 — Écriture du rattachement (UPDATE rubric_id/conf/routed_ts, rétro-compatible)

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/migrate/attach.py`
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_attach.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `migrate.route_rules.route_with_refine`, `migrate.route_llm.route_ambiguous`, `migrate.skeleton.SKELETON`/`build_skeleton`/`get_rubric_id`.
- Produces :
  - `migrate.attach.route_all(db_path, engine=None, batch_report=None) -> dict` = parcourt tous les `episodes` sans `rubric_id`, applique règles déterministes puis LLM pour l'ambigu, écrit `UPDATE episodes SET rubric_id=?, rubric_conf=?, routed_ts=?`. Retourne `{"n":..., "by_rule":..., "by_llm":..., "fallback":...}`. **N'écrit jamais dans les colonnes existantes** (ts/role/content/importance/embedding intactes) ; **enc_valence/enc_arousal restent NULL**.
  - `migrate.attach.recompute_memory_counts(db_path) -> None` = met `rubrics.memory_count`.

```python
import sqlite3, json
from migrate.skeleton import build_skeleton
from migrate.attach import route_all, recompute_memory_counts

class FakeEngine:
    def health(self): return True
    def chat(self, messages, stream=True, think=False):
        return '{"rubric_path": "divers", "conf": 0.4}'

def _rid(con, path):
    return con.execute("SELECT id FROM rubrics WHERE path=?", (path,)).fetchone()[0]

def test_route_all_attaches_and_preserves_existing(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    build_skeleton(phase1_db)
    e_paper = seed_episode("reference", "[Paper · X.pdf] titre", embedding=[0.1])
    e_refl  = seed_episode("reflection", "je repense à hier")
    e_sem   = seed_episode("semantic", "fait ambigu")  # -> LLM (fake -> divers)
    rep = route_all(phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine())
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    # paper -> savoir-applecture, conf haute
    r = con.execute("SELECT rubric_id, rubric_conf, routed_ts, enc_valence, enc_arousal, content "
                    "FROM episodes WHERE id=?", (e_paper,)).fetchone()
    assert r[0] == _rid(con, "savoir-applecture") and r[1] >= 0.95 and r[2] is not None
    assert r[3] is None and r[4] is None            # enc_* restent NULL (honnête)
    assert r[5] == "[Paper · X.pdf] titre"          # contenu intact
    assert con.execute("SELECT rubric_id FROM episodes WHERE id=?", (e_refl,)).fetchone()[0] == _rid(con, "relation-identite")
    assert con.execute("SELECT rubric_id FROM episodes WHERE id=?", (e_sem,)).fetchone()[0] == _rid(con, "divers")
    assert rep["n"] == 3 and rep["by_rule"] == 2 and rep["by_llm"] == 1

def test_route_all_is_resumable(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    build_skeleton(phase1_db)
    seed_episode("reflection", "a")
    route_all(phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine())
    rep2 = route_all(phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine())   # rien à faire
    assert rep2["n"] == 0

def test_memory_counts(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    build_skeleton(phase1_db)
    seed_episode("reflection", "x"); seed_episode("outreach", "y")
    route_all(phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine()); recompute_memory_counts(phase1_db)
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    n = con.execute("SELECT memory_count FROM rubrics WHERE path='relation-identite'").fetchone()[0]
    assert n == 2
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `migrate/attach.py` :
```python
"""Rattachement (routing) des épisodes -> rubriques. Rétro-compatible :
n'écrit QUE rubric_id/rubric_conf/routed_ts (colonnes NULLABLE Phase 1).
enc_valence/enc_arousal historiques restent NULL (§8.8). Reprenable (skip rubric_id déjà posé)."""
import sqlite3, time
from migrate.route_rules import route_with_refine
from migrate.route_llm import route_ambiguous
from migrate.skeleton import SKELETON, get_rubric_id

def _valid_paths(con):
    return [r[0] for r in con.execute("SELECT path FROM rubrics")]

def route_all(db_path, engine=None):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    valid = _valid_paths(con)
    rows = con.execute(
        "SELECT id, role, content FROM episodes WHERE rubric_id IS NULL ORDER BY id"
    ).fetchall()
    rep = {"n": 0, "by_rule": 0, "by_llm": 0, "fallback": 0}
    now = time.time()
    for eid, role, content in rows:
        path, conf = route_with_refine(role, content)
        source = "rule"
        if path is None:                          # ambigu -> LLM local
            path, conf = route_ambiguous(content, valid, engine=engine)
            source = "llm"
            if path == "divers" and conf == 0.3:
                rep["fallback"] += 1
        rid = get_rubric_id(con, path)
        if rid is None:                            # sécurité : ne jamais poser un chemin inconnu
            rid = get_rubric_id(con, "divers"); conf = min(conf, 0.3)
        con.execute(
            "UPDATE episodes SET rubric_id=?, rubric_conf=?, routed_ts=? WHERE id=?",
            (rid, float(conf), now, eid),
        )
        rep["n"] += 1
        rep["by_rule" if source == "rule" else "by_llm"] += 1
    con.commit(); con.close()
    return rep

def recompute_memory_counts(db_path):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    con.execute("UPDATE rubrics SET memory_count=0")
    for rid, n in con.execute(
        "SELECT rubric_id, COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE rubric_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY rubric_id"
    ).fetchall():
        con.execute("UPDATE rubrics SET memory_count=? WHERE id=?", (n, rid))
    con.commit(); con.close()
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces : `migrate.centroids.compute_centroids(db_path) -> int` = pour chaque rubrique ayant des épisodes rattachés (embedding non vide), calcule `centroid = np.mean(embeddings, axis=0)` et écrit `rubrics.centroid` en JSON. **Propage aussi vers les ancêtres** (un parent = moyenne des embeddings de tout son sous-arbre via `rubric_closure`). Retourne le nombre de rubriques mises à jour.

```python
import numpy as np
from migrate.skeleton import build_skeleton
from migrate.centroids import compute_centroids

def _set(con, eid, path, emb):
    rid = con.execute("SELECT id FROM rubrics WHERE path=?", (path,)).fetchone()[0]
    con.execute("UPDATE episodes SET rubric_id=?, embedding=? WHERE id=?",
                (rid, json.dumps(emb), eid)); con.commit()

def test_leaf_centroid_is_mean(phase1_db, seed_episode):

    _set(con, e1, "technique-transverse/sqlite", [0.0, 0.0])
    _set(con, e2, "technique-transverse/sqlite", [2.0, 4.0])
    n = compute_centroids(phase1_db)

        "SELECT centroid FROM rubrics WHERE path='technique-transverse/sqlite'").fetchone()[0])

    _set(con, e1, "technique-transverse/sqlite", [0.0, 0.0])
    _set(con, e2, "technique-transverse/mcp", [4.0, 4.0])

        "SELECT centroid FROM rubrics WHERE path='technique-transverse'").fetchone()[0])
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `migrate/centroids.py` :
```python
"""Centroïdes des rubriques = moyenne numpy des embeddings rattachés (§8.6).
Un parent agrège TOUT son sous-arbre via rubric_closure. Offline (numpy CPU)."""
import sqlite3, json
import numpy as np

def _embeddings_for_subtree(con, ancestor_id):
    rows = con.execute(
        "SELECT e.embedding FROM episodes e "
        "JOIN rubric_closure c ON e.rubric_id = c.descendant_id "
        "WHERE c.ancestor_id = ? AND e.embedding IS NOT NULL AND e.embedding <> '[]'",
        (ancestor_id,),
    ).fetchall()
    vecs = []
    for (emb_json,) in rows:
        try:
            v = json.loads(emb_json)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
        if v:
            vecs.append(v)
    return vecs

def compute_centroids(db_path):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    updated = 0
    for (rid,) in con.execute("SELECT id FROM rubrics").fetchall():
        vecs = _embeddings_for_subtree(con, rid)
        if not vecs:
            continue
        # aligner les dimensions (robustesse) : tronquer au min commun
        dim = min(len(v) for v in vecs)
        arr = np.asarray([v[:dim] for v in vecs], dtype=float)
        centroid = arr.mean(axis=0).tolist()
        con.execute("UPDATE rubrics SET centroid=? WHERE id=?",
                    (json.dumps(centroid), rid))
        updated += 1
    con.commit(); con.close()
    return updated
```

## Task 8 — session_id rétroactif par gaps de ts (segmentation d'événements)

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/migrate/sessions.py`
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_sessions.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `episodes.ts`, `episodes.role`, `episodes.session_id` (colonne Phase 1). `config.DREAM_HUMAN_ROLES` (§3.1 : réutilise les rôles humains pour l'inactivité).
- Produces : `migrate.sessions.SESSION_GAP_SEC` (défaut `2*3600` = 2h ; documenté comme frontière d'événement Zacks) ; `migrate.sessions.assign_sessions(db_path, gap_sec=SESSION_GAP_SEC) -> int` = attribue un `session_id` croissant (1..N) à tous les épisodes triés par `ts` ; ouvre une nouvelle session dès qu'un gap `> gap_sec` sépare deux épisodes consécutifs. Retourne le nombre de sessions. Rétro-compatible (UPDATE `session_id` uniquement). **N'écrit pas enc_valence/enc_arousal.**

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire `tests/test_sessions.py` :
```python
import sqlite3
from migrate.sessions import assign_sessions

def test_gaps_create_new_sessions(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    base = 1_700_000_000.0
    seed_episode("user", "a", ts=base + 0)
    seed_episode("assistant", "b", ts=base + 60)          # même session
    seed_episode("user", "c", ts=base + 3*3600)           # gap 3h > 2h -> nouvelle
    seed_episode("assistant", "d", ts=base + 3*3600 + 30) # même 2e session
    n = assign_sessions(phase1_db)
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    sids = [r[0] for r in con.execute("SELECT session_id FROM episodes ORDER BY ts")]
    assert sids == [1, 1, 2, 2] and n == 2

def test_single_session_when_no_gap(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    base = 1_700_000_000.0
    for i in range(5):
        seed_episode("user", str(i), ts=base + i*600)   # 10 min d'écart
    assert assign_sessions(phase1_db) == 1

def test_assign_is_idempotent(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    base = 1_700_000_000.0
    seed_episode("user", "a", ts=base)
    seed_episode("user", "b", ts=base + 5*3600)
    n1 = assign_sessions(phase1_db); n2 = assign_sessions(phase1_db)
    assert n1 == n2 == 2
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `migrate/sessions.py` :
```python
"""session_id rétroactif (§8.8) : segmentation par gaps de ts (frontières d'événement, Zacks).
Rétro-compatible : UPDATE session_id seulement. enc_valence/enc_arousal restent NULL."""
import sqlite3

SESSION_GAP_SEC = 2 * 3600  # 2h d'inactivité => nouvelle session (frontière d'événement)

def assign_sessions(db_path, gap_sec=SESSION_GAP_SEC):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    rows = con.execute("SELECT id, ts FROM episodes ORDER BY ts, id").fetchall()
    sid, prev_ts = 0, None
    for eid, ts in rows:
        if prev_ts is None or (ts - prev_ts) > gap_sec:
            sid += 1
        con.execute("UPDATE episodes SET session_id=? WHERE id=?", (sid, eid))
        prev_ts = ts
    con.commit(); con.close()
    return sid
```

## Task 9 — Validation stratifiée >=90% sur échantillon

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/migrate/validate.py`
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_validate.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `episodes.role`, `episodes.rubric_id`, `episodes.rubric_conf`, `rubrics.path`.
- Produces :
  - `migrate.validate.stratified_sample(db_path, per_role=10, seed=42) -> list[dict]` = échantillon **stratifié par rôle** (jusqu'à `per_role` par rôle, tirage déterministe via `random.Random(seed)`), chaque item `{"id","role","content","rubric_path","rubric_conf"}` — sortie destinée au contrôle humain.
  - `migrate.validate.write_sample_csv(db_path, out_path, per_role=10, seed=42) -> int` (écrit un CSV `id,role,rubric_path,conf,content,verdict` avec `verdict` vide à cocher OK/KO par un humain).
  - `migrate.validate.score_from_csv(csv_path) -> dict` = lit le CSV rempli et renvoie `{"n":..., "ok":..., "accuracy":...}` ; **la migration N'est validée que si `accuracy >= 0.90`** (§8.7).
  - `migrate.validate.PASS_THRESHOLD = 0.90`.

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire `tests/test_validate.py` :
```python
import sqlite3, csv, os
from migrate.skeleton import build_skeleton
from migrate.validate import stratified_sample, write_sample_csv, score_from_csv, PASS_THRESHOLD

def _seed_routed(phase1_db, seed_episode, role, path, n):
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    rid = con.execute("SELECT id FROM rubrics WHERE path=?", (path,)).fetchone()[0]
    for i in range(n):
        eid = seed_episode(role, f"{role}-{i}")
        con.execute("UPDATE episodes SET rubric_id=?, rubric_conf=0.9 WHERE id=?", (rid, eid))
    con.commit()

def test_sample_is_stratified(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    build_skeleton(phase1_db)
    _seed_routed(phase1_db, seed_episode, "reference", "savoir-applecture", 30)
    _seed_routed(phase1_db, seed_episode, "reflection", "relation-identite", 5)
    sample = stratified_sample(phase1_db, per_role=10)
    by_role = {}
    for it in sample:
        by_role[it["role"]] = by_role.get(it["role"], 0) + 1
    assert by_role["reference"] == 10 and by_role["reflection"] == 5

def test_score_threshold(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "s.csv"
    with open(p, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f); w.writerow(["id","role","rubric_path","conf","content","verdict"])
        for i in range(9): w.writerow([i,"x","y",0.9,"c","OK"])
        w.writerow([9,"x","y",0.9,"c","KO"])
    res = score_from_csv(str(p))
    assert res["n"] == 10 and res["ok"] == 9 and abs(res["accuracy"]-0.9) < 1e-9
    assert res["accuracy"] >= PASS_THRESHOLD

def test_write_sample_csv(phase1_db, seed_episode, tmp_path):
    build_skeleton(phase1_db)
    _seed_routed(phase1_db, seed_episode, "reference", "savoir-applecture", 3)
    out = tmp_path / "sample.csv"
    n = write_sample_csv(phase1_db, str(out), per_role=10)
    assert os.path.exists(out) and n == 3
    with open(out, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        header = f.readline().strip().split(",")
    assert header[:4] == ["id","role","rubric_path","conf"]
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `migrate/validate.py` :
```python
"""Validation stratifiée >=90% du routage (§8.7). Échantillon déterministe par rôle,
export CSV pour contrôle HUMAIN, puis score. Offline, zéro modèle."""
import sqlite3, csv, random

PASS_THRESHOLD = 0.90

def stratified_sample(db_path, per_role=10, seed=42):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    roles = [r[0] for r in con.execute(
        "SELECT DISTINCT role FROM episodes ORDER BY role")]
    out = []
    for role in roles:
        rows = con.execute(
            "SELECT e.id, e.role, e.content, r.path, e.rubric_conf "
            "FROM episodes e LEFT JOIN rubrics r ON e.rubric_id = r.id "
            "WHERE e.role=? ORDER BY e.id", (role,)).fetchall()
        rng.shuffle(rows)
        for eid, rl, content, path, conf in rows[:per_role]:
            out.append({"id": eid, "role": rl, "content": content,
                        "rubric_path": path, "rubric_conf": conf})
    con.close()
    return out

def write_sample_csv(db_path, out_path, per_role=10, seed=42):
    sample = stratified_sample(db_path, per_role=per_role, seed=seed)
    with open(out_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["id", "role", "rubric_path", "conf", "content", "verdict"])
        for it in sample:
            w.writerow([it["id"], it["role"], it["rubric_path"],
                        it["rubric_conf"], (it["content"] or "")[:200], ""])
    return len(sample)

def score_from_csv(csv_path):
    n = ok = 0
    with open(csv_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for row in csv.DictReader(f):
            v = (row.get("verdict") or "").strip().upper()
            if v not in ("OK", "KO"):
                continue
            n += 1
            if v == "OK":
                ok += 1
    acc = (ok / n) if n else 0.0
    return {"n": n, "ok": ok, "accuracy": acc}
```

## Task 10 — Orchestrateur de migration (module isolable dans modules/proposed/) + réversibilité E2E

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/modules/proposed/migrate_phase2.py` (capacité isolable, promotion humaine — §contraintes)
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_migrate_e2e.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `migrate.backup.make_backup/restore_backup`, `migrate.skeleton.build_skeleton`, `migrate.attach.route_all/recompute_memory_counts`, `migrate.centroids.compute_centroids`, `migrate.sessions.assign_sessions`, `migrate.validate.write_sample_csv`. `config.DB_PATH`.
- Produces :
  - `modules.proposed.migrate_phase2.run_migration(db_path=None, engine=None, sample_csv=None, do_backup=True) -> dict` = enchaîne backup -> skeleton -> route_all -> centroids -> sessions -> memory_counts -> écrit le CSV d'échantillon ; retourne un rapport `{"backup":..., "routed":..., "sessions":..., "rubrics_with_centroid":..., "sample_csv":...}`. **Idempotent / reprenable** (skeleton idempotent, route_all skip déjà-rattaché).
  - `modules.proposed.migrate_phase2.rollback(bak_path, db_path=None) -> None` (délègue à `restore_backup`).
  - CLI : `python -m modules.proposed.migrate_phase2 [--no-backup]` (guard : refuse si `rubrics`/colonnes Phase 1 absentes -> message « jouer Phase 0/1 d'abord »).

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire `tests/test_migrate_e2e.py` (sur DB temp, engine fake, jamais data/memory.db) :
```python
import sqlite3, os
from modules.proposed.migrate_phase2 import run_migration, rollback

class FakeEngine:
    def health(self): return True
    def chat(self, messages, stream=True, think=False):
        return '{"rubric_path":"divers","conf":0.4}'

def _mk(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    base = 1_700_000_000.0
    seed_episode("reference", "[Paper · A.pdf] t", ts=base, embedding=[1.0, 0.0])
    seed_episode("reflection", "je repense", ts=base + 30, embedding=[0.0, 1.0])
    seed_episode("reasoning", "bug fix daemon anamnese", ts=base + 60, embedding=[0.5, 0.5])
    seed_episode("semantic", "fait ambigu", ts=base + 5*3600, embedding=[0.2, 0.2])  # gap -> 2 sessions

def test_run_migration_end_to_end(phase1_db, seed_episode, tmp_path):
    _mk(phase1_db, seed_episode)
    csv_out = tmp_path / "sample.csv"
    rep = run_migration(db_path=phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine(),
                        sample_csv=str(csv_out), do_backup=True)
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    # tous rattachés
    assert con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE rubric_id IS NULL").fetchone()[0] == 0
    # 2 sessions par gap
    assert rep["sessions"] == 2
    # paper -> savoir-applecture
    path = con.execute("SELECT r.path FROM episodes e JOIN rubrics r ON e.rubric_id=r.id "
                       "WHERE e.content LIKE '[Paper%'").fetchone()[0]
    assert path == "savoir-applecture"
    # enc_* restent NULL (honnêteté)
    assert con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE enc_valence IS NOT NULL").fetchone()[0] == 0
    # centroïdes calculés (>=1)
    assert rep["rubrics_with_centroid"] >= 1
    # backup existe + CSV existe
    assert os.path.exists(rep["backup"]) and os.path.exists(str(csv_out))

def test_rollback_restores(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    _mk(phase1_db, seed_episode)
    rep = run_migration(db_path=phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine(), do_backup=True)
    # après migration, rubric_id est posé ; le backup (pré-migration) ne l'a pas
    rollback(rep["backup"], db_path=phase1_db)
    con = sqlite3.connect(phase1_db)
    # backup pris après build schema mais AVANT routage -> rubric_id NULL restauré
    assert con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE rubric_id IS NOT NULL").fetchone()[0] == 0

def test_run_migration_resumable(phase1_db, seed_episode):
    _mk(phase1_db, seed_episode)
    run_migration(db_path=phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine(), do_backup=False)
    rep2 = run_migration(db_path=phase1_db, engine=FakeEngine(), do_backup=False)
    assert rep2["routed"]["n"] == 0
```

- [ ] (3) Écrire `modules/proposed/migrate_phase2.py` :
```python
"""Migration Phase 2 (orchestrateur) — capacité ISOLABLE déposée dans modules/proposed/
(promotion humaine explicite, §contraintes). Réversible (backup) + reprenable + offline.
Enchaîne : backup -> squelette -> routage (règles+Qwen) -> centroïdes -> sessions -> counts
-> export échantillon de validation (contrôle humain >=90%)."""
import sys, sqlite3
import config
from migrate.backup import make_backup, restore_backup
from migrate.skeleton import build_skeleton
from migrate.attach import route_all, recompute_memory_counts
from migrate.centroids import compute_centroids
from migrate.sessions import assign_sessions

def _phase1_ready(db_path):
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    try:
        cols = {r[1] for r in con.execute("PRAGMA table_info(episodes)")}
        tables = {r[0] for r in con.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
    finally:
        con.close()
    need_cols = {"rubric_id", "rubric_conf", "routed_ts", "session_id", "enc_valence", "enc_arousal"}
    return need_cols <= cols and {"rubrics", "rubric_closure"} <= tables

def run_migration(db_path=None, engine=None, sample_csv=None, do_backup=True):
    db_path = str(db_path or config.DB_PATH)
    if not _phase1_ready(db_path):
        raise RuntimeError("Phase 0/1 non appliquée : colonnes/rubriques manquantes. "
                           "Jouer les migrations de schéma avant Phase 2.")
    rep = {"backup": None}
    if do_backup:
        rep["backup"] = make_backup(db_path)
    build_skeleton(db_path)
    rep["routed"] = route_all(db_path, engine=engine)
    rep["rubrics_with_centroid"] = compute_centroids(db_path)
    rep["sessions"] = assign_sessions(db_path)
    recompute_memory_counts(db_path)
    if sample_csv:
        from migrate.validate import write_sample_csv
        rep["sample_csv"] = sample_csv
        write_sample_csv(db_path, sample_csv)
    return rep

def rollback(bak_path, db_path=None):
    restore_backup(bak_path, str(db_path or config.DB_PATH))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    do_backup = "--no-backup" not in sys.argv
    r = run_migration(do_backup=do_backup, sample_csv=str(config.DATA_DIR / "phase2_sample.csv"))
    print("Migration Phase 2 terminée :", r)
    print("-> Remplir la colonne 'verdict' du CSV puis valider avec migrate.validate.score_from_csv")
```

## Task 11 — Dry-run offline sur une COPIE de la vraie DB (garde-fou avant prod)

**Files:**
- Create: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_real_db_dryrun.py` (test opt-in, marqué `skip` par défaut si copie absente)
- (aucune modif de code prod)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : tout `migrate.*` + `modules.proposed.migrate_phase2.run_migration`. **N'écrit jamais `data/memory.db`** : opère sur une **copie** faite dans `tmp_path`, à laquelle on ajoute d'abord le schéma Phase 0/1 (via `schema_phase1.sql` appliqué en `ALTER`/`CREATE` sur la copie).
- Produces : preuve empirique que la migration route les 1172 réels avec `by_rule` couvrant reference/reflection/outreach/dream/thought/reasoning (~984 attendus déterministes) et que seuls semantic(174)+episodic(8)=182 passent au LLM/fallback.

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire `tests/test_real_db_dryrun.py` :
```python
import os, sqlite3, shutil, pathlib
import pytest

REAL = pathlib.Path("/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/data/memory.db")
SCHEMA = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / "schema_phase1.sql"

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(not REAL.exists(), reason="vraie DB absente")

class FakeEngine:  # offline : pas d'appel Ollama pendant le dry-run CI
    def health(self): return True
    def chat(self, messages, stream=True, think=False):
        return '{"rubric_path":"divers","conf":0.4}'

def _copy_and_upgrade(tmp_path):
    dst = tmp_path / "copy.db"
    # checkpoint + copie (ne touche pas l'original)
    src = sqlite3.connect(str(REAL)); src.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)"); src.close()
    shutil.copy2(REAL, dst)
    con = sqlite3.connect(str(dst))
    # appliquer le schéma additif Phase 0/1 sur la copie (ADD COLUMN si absent)
    existing = {r[1] for r in con.execute("PRAGMA table_info(episodes)")}
    for col, decl in [("rubric_id","INTEGER"),("rubric_conf","REAL"),("routed_ts","REAL"),
                      ("session_id","INTEGER"),("enc_valence","REAL"),("enc_arousal","REAL"),
                      ("last_recall_ts","REAL"),("recall_count","INTEGER DEFAULT 0")]:
        if col not in existing:
            con.execute(f"ALTER TABLE episodes ADD COLUMN {col} {decl}")
    con.executescript(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rubrics (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL, "
        "label TEXT, parent_id INTEGER, kind_hint TEXT, centroid TEXT, depth INTEGER DEFAULT 0, "
        "memory_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_ts REAL);"
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rubric_closure (ancestor_id INTEGER NOT NULL, "
        "descendant_id INTEGER NOT NULL, depth INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(ancestor_id,descendant_id));")
    con.commit(); con.close()
    return str(dst)

def test_dryrun_on_real_copy(tmp_path):
    from modules.proposed.migrate_phase2 import run_migration
    dst = _copy_and_upgrade(tmp_path)
    rep = run_migration(db_path=dst, engine=FakeEngine(), do_backup=False)
    con = sqlite3.connect(dst)
    # 0 non rattaché
    assert con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE rubric_id IS NULL").fetchone()[0] == 0
    total = con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes").fetchone()[0]
    assert rep["routed"]["n"] == total
    # déterministe couvre reference+reflection+outreach+dream+thought+reasoning
    detn = con.execute(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE role IN "
        "('reference','reflection','outreach','dream','thought','reasoning')").fetchone()[0]
    assert rep["routed"]["by_rule"] == detn                 # ~984
    # LLM/fallback = semantic + episodic
    assert rep["routed"]["by_llm"] == total - detn          # ~182
    # tous les papers -> savoir-applecture
    bad = con.execute(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes e JOIN rubrics r ON e.rubric_id=r.id "
        "WHERE e.content LIKE '[Paper · %' AND r.path NOT LIKE 'savoir-applecture%'").fetchone()[0]
    assert bad == 0
    # enc_* jamais écrits
    assert con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes WHERE enc_valence IS NOT NULL").fetchone()[0] == 0
```

```bash
# 4. En cas de problème : rollback complet
```

## Garde-fous transverses (rappel spec, présents dans chaque tâche)
- Rétro-compatible : uniquement lecture + `UPDATE` de colonnes NULLABLE créées en Phase 1 (`rubric_id/rubric_conf/routed_ts/session_id`) + nouvelles lignes `rubrics`/`rubric_closure`. `ts/role/content/importance/embedding` jamais touchés. `enc_valence/enc_arousal` jamais écrits (NULL honnête).
- Réversible : `make_backup` avant toute écriture ; `rollback`/`restore_backup` testés (T1, T10).
- Offline/gratuit : routage déterministe (zéro modèle) + embeddings déjà en DB (numpy CPU) + Qwen 9B **local** via `engine.py` (avec fallback si Ollama down) ; tests **mockent** l'engine (aucun réseau en CI).
- Isolable : orchestrateur dans `modules/proposed/` (promotion humaine explicite), le reste en package `migrate/` importable.
- Fidélité = validé-reproduit : aucun affect passé reconstruit.

# Phase 3 — Temporalité (sessions, landmarks, circadien, contiguïté TCM, felt_time)
*Fichiers : /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/conftest.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_schema_temporal.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_circadian.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_affect_circadian.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_temporal_contig.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_felt_time.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_sessions_landmarks.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_mcp_temporal.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/config.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/memory.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/modules/proposed/circadian.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/affect.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/salience.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/mcp_server.py, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/requirements-dev.txt, /Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/data/memory.db.bak-2026-07-01*
Phase 3 — Temporalité. Plan TDD bite-sized, ancré sur le VRAI code (`memory.py`, `affect.py`, `salience.py`, `config.py`, `modules/clock.py`, `mcp_server.py`). Branche courante : `anamnese-2.0-cerveau`. Python : `.venv/bin/python` (3.14.3). Tests : `pytest` sur DB temporaire (`tmp_path`), jamais `data/memory.db`.

CONTRAINTES RAPPELÉES DANS CHAQUE TASK : (a) rétro-compatible — colonnes NULLABLE + `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + `ADD COLUMN` gardé, aucune écriture existante ne casse ; (b) réversible — backup `data/memory.db.bak-2026-07-01` (Task 0) ; (c) 100% offline/gratuit — aucune dépendance réseau, embeddings model2vec CPU, LLM local Qwen via Ollama non sollicité ici ; (d) s'appuyer sur `salience.py` pour le scoring et `modules/registry.py` (dépôt `proposed/`) pour la capacité isolable (circadien) ; (e) fidélité = validé-reproduit — `felt_time` étiqueté "représentation modélisée", pas ressenti/qualia.

### Task 1 — Schéma temporel : tables `sessions`/`landmarks` + colonne `episodes.session_id` (idempotent, rétro-compatible)

**Files:**
- Modify `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/memory.py` — `_init_schema` (memory.py:30-59) : ajouter les CREATE et un ADD COLUMN gardé.
- Test: `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_schema_temporal.py` (étoffe Task 0).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `Memory._init_schema()` (memory.py:30), `self.db.executescript`/`execute` (sqlite3).
- Produces : table `sessions(id INTEGER PK, started_ts REAL NOT NULL, ended_ts REAL, summary TEXT, rubric_focus INTEGER, gap_before_h REAL)` ; table `landmarks(id INTEGER PK, ts REAL NOT NULL, label TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT, salience REAL DEFAULT 0.5)` ; colonne `episodes.session_id INTEGER` (NULLABLE) ; index `idx_episodes_session`.

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire le test qui échoue — ajouter à `tests/test_schema_temporal.py` :
```python
def _cols(mem, table):
    return {r[1] for r in mem.db.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})").fetchall()}


def test_sessions_table_shape(mem):
    cols = _cols(mem, "sessions")
    assert cols == {"id", "started_ts", "ended_ts", "summary", "rubric_focus", "gap_before_h"}


def test_landmarks_table_shape(mem):
    cols = _cols(mem, "landmarks")
    assert cols == {"id", "ts", "label", "kind", "salience"}


def test_episodes_has_nullable_session_id(mem):
    info = {r[1]: r for r in mem.db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(episodes)").fetchall()}
    assert "session_id" in info
    # colonne NULLABLE (notnull == 0) -> rétro-compat : les écritures existantes ne cassent pas
    assert info["session_id"][3] == 0


def test_add_column_is_idempotent(mem):
    # ré-initialiser le schéma une 2e fois ne doit pas lever "duplicate column"
    mem._init_schema()
    assert "session_id" in _cols(mem, "episodes")


def test_legacy_add_still_works_without_session(mem):
    # une écriture "à l'ancienne" (Memory.add) reste valide : session_id NULL
    assert mem.add("user", "bonjour", embedding=[0.1, 0.2]) is True
    row = mem.db.execute("SELECT session_id FROM episodes ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    assert row[0] is None
```

```python
                ts       REAL NOT NULL,
                label    TEXT NOT NULL,
                kind     TEXT,
                salience REAL DEFAULT 0.5
            );
            """
        )
        # ADD COLUMN idempotent (SQLite ne connaît pas ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS) :
        # garde par PRAGMA -> ne casse aucune DB déjà migrée, ni la DB live 6-colonnes.
        ep_cols = {r[1] for r in self.db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(episodes)").fetchall()}
        if "session_id" not in ep_cols:
            self.db.execute("ALTER TABLE episodes ADD COLUMN session_id INTEGER")
        self.db.execute(
            "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_episodes_session ON episodes(session_id)"
        )
```
(Note : le `sessions.id` est déclaré `AUTOINCREMENT` pour cohérence avec `episodes`/`skills` ; le schéma spec §3.1 dit `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY` — équivalent fonctionnel, colonnes identiques. Le test vérifie l'ensemble des colonnes, pas AUTOINCREMENT.)

```python
import time

import pytest

import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "modules" / "proposed"))
import circadian  # noqa: E402


def test_cortisol_peaks_morning_troughs_night():
    # cortisol : haut le matin (~8h), bas la nuit (~2h). Cf. de Kloet, rythme HPA.
    assert circadian.cortisol_baseline(8.0) > circadian.cortisol_baseline(2.0)
    assert circadian.cortisol_baseline(8.0) > circadian.cortisol_baseline(20.0)


def test_melatonin_high_night_low_day():
    # mélatonine : quasi nulle en journée, haute la nuit (DLMO ~21h). Marqueur SCN.
    assert circadian.melatonin_baseline(3.0) > circadian.melatonin_baseline(14.0)
    assert circadian.melatonin_baseline(14.0) < 0.15


def test_baselines_in_unit_range():
    for h in range(24):
        c = circadian.cortisol_baseline(float(h))
        m = circadian.melatonin_baseline(float(h))
        assert 0.0 <= c <= 1.0
        assert 0.0 <= m <= 1.0


def test_anti_phase_cortisol_melatonin():
    # cortisol et mélatonine sont grossièrement en OPPOSITION de phase (pendant nocturne).
    assert circadian.cortisol_baseline(8.0) > circadian.melatonin_baseline(8.0)
    assert circadian.melatonin_baseline(2.0) > circadian.cortisol_baseline(2.0)


def test_baselines_now_uses_given_timestamp():
    # 03:00 heure locale -> mélatonine > cortisol
    t = time.mktime(time.struct_time((2026, 7, 1, 3, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1)))
    b = circadian.baselines_now(now=t)
    assert set(b) == {"cortisol", "melatonine"}
    assert b["melatonine"] > b["cortisol"]
```

- [ ] (3) Implémentation minimale — `modules/proposed/circadian.py` :
```python
"""circadian.py — baselines humorales circadiennes = f(heure locale). [solide]

Rythme SCN validé-reproduit : cortisol pic matinal (~8h) / creux nocturne (HPA,
de Kloet) ; mélatonine ~nulle en journée, haute la nuit (DLMO ~20-21h30). Ce module
est un CORRÉLAT physiologique honnête du "moment de la journée" — pas un vécu.

Isolable (dépôt modules/proposed/) : importé explicitement par affect.py, jamais
chargé automatiquement par registry.load_modules. 100% offline (stdlib pure).
"""
import math
import time

# amplitudes/plancher [heuristique] : la biologie donne le signe + l'ordre de grandeur.
_CORTISOL_MIN, _CORTISOL_AMP = 0.10, 0.55   # creux ~0.10, pic ~0.65
_MELATONIN_MIN, _MELATONIN_AMP = 0.02, 0.75  # jour ~0.02, nuit ~0.77
_CORTISOL_PEAK_H = 8.0     # acrophase cortisol ~08:00
_MELATONIN_PEAK_H = 3.0    # acrophase mélatonine ~03:00 (nuit profonde)


def _cosine_phase(hour_local, peak_h):
    """Cosinor 24h ∈ [0,1] : 1 au pic (peak_h), 0 à l'anti-pic (peak_h+12)."""
    ang = 2.0 * math.pi * (float(hour_local) - peak_h) / 24.0
    return (1.0 + math.cos(ang)) / 2.0


def cortisol_baseline(hour_local):
    return _CORTISOL_MIN + _CORTISOL_AMP * _cosine_phase(hour_local, _CORTISOL_PEAK_H)


def melatonin_baseline(hour_local):
    return _MELATONIN_MIN + _MELATONIN_AMP * _cosine_phase(hour_local, _MELATONIN_PEAK_H)


def baselines_now(now=None):
    """Baselines cortisol/mélatonine à l'HEURE LOCALE de `now` (ou maintenant)."""
    lt = time.localtime(now if now is not None else time.time())
    h = lt.tm_hour + lt.tm_min / 60.0
    return {"cortisol": cortisol_baseline(h), "melatonine": melatonin_baseline(h)}
```
Vérif : à 8h, cosine_phase(8,8)=1 → cortisol≈0.65 ; cosine_phase(8,3)=cos(2π·5/24)=cos(75°)≈0.259 → mélatonine≈0.02+0.75·0.629... recalcul : (1+cos(75°))/2=(1+0.259)/2=0.629 → 0.02+0.75·0.629=0.49 ? Vérifier `test_melatonin_high_night_low_day` : melatonin(14) : (1+cos(2π·11/24))/2 = (1+cos(165°))/2=(1-0.966)/2=0.017 → 0.02+0.75·0.017=0.033 < 0.15 OK ; melatonin(3)=(1+cos0)/2=1 → 0.77 > 0.033 OK. `test_anti_phase` melatonin(2)=(1+cos(2π·-1/24))/2=(1+cos15°)/2=0.983→0.77·0.983+0.02=0.76 ; cortisol(2)=(1+cos(2π·-6/24))/2=(1+cos90°)/2=0.5→0.10+0.55·0.5=0.375 → melatonin(2)>cortisol(2) OK. cortisol(8)=0.65 > melatonin(8)=0.49 OK.

```python
import time

import affect


def test_melatonine_hormone_registered():
    assert "melatonine" in affect.HORMONES
    base, half = affect.HORMONES["melatonine"]
    assert 0.0 <= base <= 1.0 and half > 0


def test_get_state_includes_melatonine(mem):
    s = affect.get_state(mem)
    assert "melatonine" in s
    # les 7 hormones historiques restent présentes (rétro-compat)
    for h in ("dopamine", "serotonine", "noradrenaline", "cortisol",
              "ocytocine", "acetylcholine", "endorphine"):
        assert h in s


def test_get_state_uses_circadian_baseline_for_cortisol(mem):
    # base KV vide -> get_state retombe sur baseline. À 8h le baseline cortisol
    # circadien (~0.65) est BIEN au-dessus de l'ancien constant 0.2.
    t_morning = time.mktime(time.struct_time((2026, 7, 1, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1)))
    t_night = time.mktime(time.struct_time((2026, 7, 1, 2, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1)))
    s_morn = affect.get_state(mem, now=t_morning)
    s_night = affect.get_state(mem, now=t_night)
    assert s_morn["cortisol"] > s_night["cortisol"]
    assert s_night["melatonine"] > s_morn["melatonine"]


def test_saved_level_still_decays_toward_circadian_base(mem):
    # un pic de cortisol récent reste > baseline mais converge vers le baseline circadien
    t = time.mktime(time.struct_time((2026, 7, 1, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1)))
    mem.kv_set("affect", '{"cortisol": 0.9, "_ts": %d}' % int(t))
    s = affect.get_state(mem, now=t)  # âge 0 -> niveau brut conservé
    assert abs(s["cortisol"] - 0.9) < 1e-6


def test_legacy_kv_without_melatonine_falls_back(mem):
    # KV écrit par l'ancien code (7 hormones, pas de melatonine) -> pas de crash
    t = time.mktime(time.struct_time((2026, 7, 1, 3, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1)))
    mem.kv_set("affect", '{"dopamine": 0.5, "_ts": %d}' % int(t))
    s = affect.get_state(mem, now=t)
    assert "melatonine" in s and s["melatonine"] > 0.5  # baseline nocturne
```

```python
# Circadien (module isolable dans modules/proposed/) : baselines cortisol/mélatonine
# = f(heure locale). Import DIRECT (pas via registry) ; si absent -> baselines statiques.
try:
    import os as _os
    import importlib.util as _ilu
    _CIRC_PATH = _os.path.join(_os.path.dirname(__file__), "modules", "proposed", "circadian.py")
    _spec = _ilu.spec_from_file_location("circadian", _CIRC_PATH)
    circadian = _ilu.module_from_spec(_spec)
    _spec.loader.exec_module(circadian)
except Exception:  # module absent/cassé : on garde les baselines constantes de HORMONES
    circadian = None


def _baselines(now):
    """Baseline de chaque hormone : circadien pour cortisol/mélatonine, constant sinon."""
    base = {h: b for h, (b, _half) in HORMONES.items()}
    if circadian is not None:
        try:
            base.update(circadian.baselines_now(now))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return base
```
Réécrire `get_state` (affect.py:42-54) pour accepter `now` et utiliser les baselines circadiennes comme point d'attraction du decay :
```python
def get_state(mem, now=None):
    """Niveaux actuels (après décroissance vers baseline depuis la dernière maj).

    Le baseline de cortisol/mélatonine est CIRCADIEN (f(heure locale)) ; les autres
    hormones gardent leur baseline constant. Le baseline n'est jamais persisté (calculé
    à la volée) — sinon on figerait l'heure dans le KV.
    """
    now = now or time.time()
    base = _baselines(now)
    raw = mem.kv_get("affect")
    if not raw:
        return dict(base)
    data = json.loads(raw)
    age_h = max(0.0, (now - data.get("_ts", now)) / 3600.0)
    state = {}
    for h, (_b, half) in HORMONES.items():
        b = base[h]
        lvl = data.get(h, b)
        state[h] = _clamp(b + (lvl - b) * (0.5 ** (age_h / half)))
    return state
```
(`_save`, `event`, `valence_arousal`, `salience_boost`, `describe` restent inchangés : ils itèrent sur `HORMONES`/`get_state` et tolèrent la 8e hormone ; `valence_arousal`/`salience_boost` n'utilisent pas `melatonine`, donc pas de régression de valence.)

**Interfaces:**

- Produces : `config.W_TEMPORAL_CONTIG = 0.25`, `config.TEMPORAL_CONTIG_HALFLIFE_H = 24.0` ; `salience.temporal_contig(delta_ts, halflife_h=config.TEMPORAL_CONTIG_HALFLIFE_H) -> float` (0..1, décroît avec |Δts|) ; `Memory.retrieve(..., anchor_ts=None)` — ajoute `W_TEMPORAL_CONTIG*contig` uniquement si `anchor_ts` fourni (neutre sinon = rétro-compat totale).

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire le test qui échoue — `tests/test_temporal_contig.py` :
```python
import time

import config
import salience


def test_temporal_contig_decreases_with_gap():
    now = 0.0
    close = salience.temporal_contig(3600.0)      # 1h
    far = salience.temporal_contig(3600.0 * 72)   # 72h
    assert 0.0 <= far < close <= 1.0
    assert salience.temporal_contig(0.0) == 1.0


def test_temporal_contig_symmetric():
    assert salience.temporal_contig(3600.0) == salience.temporal_contig(-3600.0)


def test_config_weights_present():
    assert config.W_TEMPORAL_CONTIG > 0
    assert config.TEMPORAL_CONTIG_HALFLIFE_H > 0


def test_retrieve_without_anchor_is_unchanged(mem):
    # rétro-compat : sans anchor_ts, retrieve se comporte comme avant
    now = time.time()
    mem.add("user", "modelisation 3d camera", embedding=[1.0, 0.0])
    mem.add("user", "recette de cuisine", embedding=[0.0, 1.0])
    res = mem.retrieve("modelisation 3d", [1.0, 0.0], now=now, top_k=2)
    assert res[0][3] == "modelisation 3d camera"  # content en index 3


def test_retrieve_with_anchor_boosts_temporal_neighbor(mem):
    now = time.time()
    # deux épisodes NON pertinents lexicalement/semantiquement pour la requête,
    # mais l'un est temporellement collé à l'ancre.
    mem.db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO episodes (ts, role, content, importance, embedding) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
        (now - 3600.0, "user", "voisin proche", 0.5, "[0.0, 1.0]"))
    mem.db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO episodes (ts, role, content, importance, embedding) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
        (now - 3600.0 * 200, "user", "voisin lointain", 0.5, "[0.0, 1.0]"))
    mem.db.commit()
    res = mem.retrieve("xyzzy", [1.0, 0.0], now=now, top_k=2, anchor_ts=now)
    # le voisin temporel proche remonte au-dessus du lointain
    contents = [r[3] for r in res]
    assert contents.index("voisin proche") < contents.index("voisin lointain")
```

`config.py`, après la ligne `RECENCY_HALFLIFE_HOURS = 72.0` (config.py:31) :
```python

# --- Temporalité (Phase 3) : contiguïté temporelle TCM/CRP (Howard-Kahana) [solide] ---
W_TEMPORAL_CONTIG          = 0.25   # poids du voisinage temporel au rappel
TEMPORAL_CONTIG_HALFLIFE_H = 24.0   # demi-vie de la contiguïté (|Δts| en heures)
SESSION_GAP_H              = 6.0    # inactivité HUMAINE (h) ouvrant une nouvelle session (Zacks)
FELT_TIME_HALFLIFE_H       = 12.0   # felt_time : demi-vie de l'écart depuis dernière interaction
```
`salience.py`, ajouter après `recency` (salience.py:37) :
```python
def temporal_contig(delta_ts, halflife_h=config.TEMPORAL_CONTIG_HALFLIFE_H):
    """Contiguïté temporelle (TCM/CRP, Kahana) : 1 à Δt=0, décroît avec |Δts| (secondes).

    Effet de contiguïté : ce qu'on a fait juste avant/après remonte ensemble. Symétrique.
    """
    gap_h = abs(float(delta_ts)) / 3600.0
    return 0.5 ** (gap_h / halflife_h)
```
`memory.py`, `retrieve` (memory.py:107) — signature + terme. Remplacer l'en-tête et la boucle de scoring :
```python
    def retrieve(self, query_text, query_emb, now=None, top_k=config.TOP_K, kinds=None,
                 anchor_ts=None):
        """Récupération HYBRIDE sparse : sémantique + lexical + saillance + (option) contiguïté.

        kinds     : si fourni (ex. ['reasoning']), ne garde que ces types.
        anchor_ts : si fourni, booste les voisins TEMPORELS de cette ancre (TCM/CRP).
        """
        now = now or time.time()
        rows = self.db.execute(
            "SELECT ts, role, content, importance, embedding FROM episodes"
        ).fetchall()
        scored = []
        for ts, role, content, importance, emb_json in rows:
            if kinds and role not in kinds:
                continue
            emb = json.loads(emb_json) if emb_json else None
            rel = (
                salience.cosine(query_emb, emb)
                if (query_emb and emb and len(emb) == len(query_emb))
                else 0.0
            )
            lex = salience.lexical_overlap(query_text, content)
            rec = salience.recency(ts, now)
            s = (
                config.W_RELEVANCE * rel
                + config.W_LEXICAL * lex
                + config.W_RECENCY * rec
                + config.W_IMPORTANCE * float(importance)
            )
            contig = 0.0
            if anchor_ts is not None:
                contig = salience.temporal_contig(ts - anchor_ts)
                s += config.W_TEMPORAL_CONTIG * contig
            scored.append(
                (
                    s,
                    ts,
                    role,
                    content,
                    {"rel": round(rel, 3), "lex": round(lex, 3),
                     "rec": round(rec, 3), "contig": round(contig, 3)},
                )
            )
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        return scored[:top_k]
```

Steps :
- [ ] (1) Écrire le test qui échoue — `tests/test_felt_time.py` :
```python
import time

import affect


def test_felt_time_zero_when_no_history(mem):
    # aucune interaction humaine passée -> pas d'écart mesurable -> 0.0 (neutre, honnête)
    assert affect.felt_time(mem, now=time.time()) == 0.0


def test_felt_time_grows_with_gap(mem):
    now = time.time()
    # dernière interaction HUMAINE il y a 1h vs il y a 100h
    mem.db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO episodes (ts, role, content, importance, embedding) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
        (now - 3600.0, "user", "salut", 0.5, "[]"))
    mem.db.commit()
    ft_recent = affect.felt_time(mem, now=now)

    m2_now = now
    mem2 = affect  # placeholder to avoid confusion; use same mem, move ts
    mem.db.execute("UPDATE episodes SET ts=?", (now - 3600.0 * 100,))
    mem.db.commit()
    ft_old = affect.felt_time(mem, now=now)

    assert 0.0 <= ft_recent < ft_old <= 1.0


def test_felt_time_ignores_non_human_roles(mem):
    now = time.time()
    # seuls user/assistant comptent (DREAM_HUMAN_ROLES) ; un 'dream' récent ne compte pas
    mem.db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO episodes (ts, role, content, importance, embedding) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
        (now - 1.0, "dream", "reve", 0.5, "[]"))
    mem.db.commit()
    assert affect.felt_time(mem, now=now) == 0.0


def test_felt_time_bounded():
    class _FakeMem:
        class _DB:
            def execute(self, *a, **k):
                class _C:
                    def fetchone(self_inner):
                        return (0.0,)  # dernière interaction à l'epoch -> écart énorme
                return _C()
        db = _DB()
        def kv_get(self, *a, **k):
            return None
    v = affect.felt_time(_FakeMem(), now=time.time())
    assert 0.0 <= v <= 1.0
```

```python
def felt_time(mem, now=None):
    """felt_time — "temps ressenti" [heuristique, persona assumé].

    Scalaire ∈ [0,1] dérivé de l'écart depuis la DERNIÈRE interaction humaine
    (rôles user/assistant). 0 = on vient de se parler ; → 1 = long silence.
    Module l'intensité des "retrouvailles" et la cadence perçue.

    ATTENTION HONNÊTETÉ : représentation MODÉLISÉE du temps écoulé, jamais un ressenti
    conscient. Alimente le persona / des events (retrouvailles, cadence), PAS la valence pure.
    """
    import config
    now = now or time.time()
    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in config.DREAM_HUMAN_ROLES)
    row = mem.db.execute(
        f"SELECT MAX(ts) FROM episodes WHERE role IN ({placeholders})",
        tuple(config.DREAM_HUMAN_ROLES),
    ).fetchone()
    last = row[0] if row else None
    if last is None:
        return 0.0
    gap_h = max(0.0, (now - float(last)) / 3600.0)
    # saturation douce : 1 - 0.5^(gap/halflife) -> 0 à Δt=0, tend vers 1.
    return _clamp(1.0 - 0.5 ** (gap_h / config.FELT_TIME_HALFLIFE_H))
```

```python
import time


def test_session_begin_returns_id_and_sets_current(mem):
    sid = mem.session_begin(summary="reprise 3d", now=1000.0)
    assert isinstance(sid, int) and sid > 0
    assert mem.current_session_id() == sid
    row = mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT started_ts, ended_ts, summary FROM sessions WHERE id=?", (sid,)).fetchone()
    assert row[0] == 1000.0 and row[1] is None and row[2] == "reprise 3d"


def test_add_attaches_to_current_session(mem):

    mem.add("user", "on continue la camera 3d", embedding=[])
    got = mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT session_id FROM episodes ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()[0]
    assert got == sid


def test_add_without_session_leaves_null(mem):

    assert mem.add("user", "hors session", embedding=[]) is True
    got = mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT session_id FROM episodes ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()[0]
    assert got is None


def test_session_end_closes_and_clears_current(mem):

    ended = mem.session_end(now=2000.0)
    assert ended == sid
    assert mem.current_session_id() is None

        "SELECT ended_ts FROM sessions WHERE id=?", (sid,)).fetchone()[0] == 2000.0

    mem.session_end(now=3600.0)                 # ferme à +1h
    s2 = mem.session_begin(now=3600.0 + 7200.0) # rouvre 2h après la fermeture
    gap = mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT gap_before_h FROM sessions WHERE id=?", (s2,)).fetchone()[0]
    assert abs(gap - 2.0) < 1e-6


def test_landmark_add(mem):

    row = mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT ts, label, kind, salience FROM landmarks WHERE id=?", (lid,)).fetchone()
    assert row == (500.0, "pré-lancement beta", "jalon projet", 0.8)


def test_landmark_salience_clamped(mem):

        "SELECT salience FROM landmarks WHERE id=?", (lid,)).fetchone()[0] == 1.0
```

`memory.py`, ajouter à la fin de la classe (après `kv_set`, memory.py:195) :
```python
    # --- sessions (segmentation d'événements, Phase 3) ---
    def current_session_id(self):
        v = self.kv_get("current_session_id")
        return int(v) if v is not None else None

    def session_begin(self, summary=None, now=None):
        """Ouvre une session (frontière d'événement). Ferme d'abord une session pendante.

        gap_before_h = heures écoulées depuis la fermeture de la session précédente
        (intensité des "retrouvailles").
        """
        now = now or time.time()
        if self.current_session_id() is not None:
            self.session_end(now=now)
        prev = self.db.execute(
            "SELECT ended_ts, started_ts FROM sessions ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        ).fetchone()
        gap_before_h = None
        if prev is not None:
            ref = prev[0] if prev[0] is not None else prev[1]
            if ref is not None:
                gap_before_h = max(0.0, (now - float(ref)) / 3600.0)
        cur = self.db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO sessions (started_ts, summary, gap_before_h) VALUES (?,?,?)",
            (now, summary, gap_before_h),
        )
        self.db.commit()
        sid = int(cur.lastrowid)
        self.kv_set("current_session_id", str(sid))
        return sid

    def session_end(self, now=None):
        """Ferme la session courante (pose ended_ts). No-op si aucune n'est ouverte."""
        sid = self.current_session_id()
        if sid is None:
            return None
        now = now or time.time()
        self.db.execute("UPDATE sessions SET ended_ts=? WHERE id=?", (now, sid))
        self.db.execute("DELETE FROM kv WHERE key='current_session_id'")
        self.db.commit()
        return sid

    # --- landmarks (jalons temporels de référence) ---
    def landmark_add(self, label, kind=None, salience=0.5, now=None):
        now = now or time.time()
        sal = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(salience)))
        cur = self.db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO landmarks (ts, label, kind, salience) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
            (now, label, kind, sal),
        )
        self.db.commit()
        return int(cur.lastrowid)
```
(`session_end` supprime la clé kv via DELETE plutôt que `kv_set(...,None)` car `kv.value` est `NOT NULL` — cf. schéma memory.py:52-56.)

```python
import importlib

import pytest

import memory


@pytest.fixture
def srv(raw_db, monkeypatch):
    import mcp_server
    importlib.reload(mcp_server)
    # rediriger le Memory du serveur vers une DB temporaire (jamais data/memory.db)

    return mcp_server


def _call(tool):
    # FastMCP garde la fonction d'origine accessible pour les tests
    return getattr(tool, "fn", tool)


def test_mcp_session_begin_then_end(srv):

    assert "session" in out.lower()
    assert srv._mem.current_session_id() is not None
    out2 = _call(srv.session_end)()
    assert "clos" in out2.lower() or "ferm" in out2.lower() or "session" in out2.lower()

def test_mcp_session_begin_reports_felt_time(srv):
```

```python
@mcp.tool()
def session_begin(summary: str = "") -> str:
    """Ouvre une SESSION (frontière d'événement, segmentation Zacks). Optionnel : un résumé.

    Calcule les "retrouvailles" (écart depuis la dernière interaction) — représentation
    MODÉLISÉE du temps écoulé, pas un ressenti conscient.
    """
    ft = affect.felt_time(_mem)
    sid = _mem.session_begin(summary=summary or None)
    if ft >= 0.5:
        affect.event(_mem, "chaleur_user", intensity=min(1.0, ft))
    return (f"Session #{sid} ouverte"
            + (f" — résumé : {summary}" if summary else "")
            + f" (felt_time des retrouvailles : {ft:.2f} — représentation modélisée).")


@mcp.tool()
def session_end() -> str:
    """Clôt la session courante (pose sa borne de fin). Sans effet si aucune n'est ouverte."""
    sid = _mem.session_end()
    if sid is None:
        return "Aucune session ouverte à clore."
    return f"Session #{sid} close."


@mcp.tool()
def landmark_add(label: str, kind: str = "", salience: float = 0.5) -> str:
    """Pose un JALON temporel de référence (ex. 'pré-lancement beta', 'bascule Fable 5').

    kind: 'jalon projet' / 'perso' / 'événement'. salience: 0..1 (importance du repère).
    """
    lid = _mem.landmark_add(label, kind=kind or None, salience=salience)
    return f"Jalon #{lid} posé : « {label} »" + (f" [{kind}]" if kind else "") + "."
```

# Phase 3.5 — Facettes (au pixel près)
*Fichiers : tests/*.py, facets_extract.py, auto_split.py, memory.py, salience.py, config.py, mcp_server.py, requirements.txt, scripts/migrate_facets.py*
> **Phase 3.5 — Facettes (« au pixel près »)**. Précision combinatoire par intersection de traits (feature-based), en complément de la profondeur d'arbre. Périmètre : tables `facets`/`episode_facets` ; extraction auto des facettes candidates (mots-clés saillants + entités techniques) à l'écriture ET à la migration, dédup contre `facets.slug` ; terme `W_FACET * facet_overlap` (Jaccard pondéré) dans le rappel ; garde-fous d'arbre (profondeur max 6, `SPLIT_MAX=12`, auto-split k-means offline en numpy pur).
>
> **Contraintes rappelées dans CHAQUE task** : (a) **rétro-compatible** — uniquement nouvelles tables + colonnes NULLABLE, aucune écriture existante ne casse ; (b) **réversible** — `cp data/memory.db data/memory.db.bak-YYYYMMDD` avant migration ; (c) **100% offline/gratuit** — model2vec CPU, k-means numpy pur (pas de sklearn), aucun cloud ; (d) **s'appuyer sur `salience.py`** pour le scoring et **`modules/proposed/`** pour l'isolable ; (e) **fidélité = validé-reproduit** (facettes = intersection de traits = *feature-based localization*, pas de sentience).
>
> **État réel constaté (ancrage code)** : `grep` sur `.py` (hors `.venv`) → AUCUN `rubric`/`facet`/`session_id`/`W_FACET`. Phases 0/1/3 ne sont PAS encore en code, seule la spec existe (branche `anamnese-2.0-cerveau`, dernier commit `3fbe692`). → **Phase 3.5 est conçue AUTONOME** : `episode_facets.episode_id` référence `episodes(id)` qui existe déjà (`memory.py:38`), sans dépendre de la table `rubrics`. Si Phase 1 est jouée avant/après, zéro collision (objets distincts).

```python
def _table_cols(mem, table):
    return [r[1] for r in mem.db.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})").fetchall()]


def test_facets_tables_created(mem):
    tables = {r[0] for r in mem.db.execute(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").fetchall()}
    assert "facets" in tables
    assert "episode_facets" in tables
    assert _table_cols(mem, "facets") == ["id", "slug"]
    assert _table_cols(mem, "episode_facets") == ["episode_id", "facet_id", "weight"]


def test_facets_slug_unique(mem):
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Migration Phase 3.5 — facets/episode_facets. RÉVERSIBLE : backup avant.

Usage : ./.venv/bin/python scripts/migrate_facets.py
Additif (IF NOT EXISTS) : aucune écriture existante ne casse.

import datetime
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
import config  # noqa: E402
from memory import Memory  # noqa: E402


def main():
    db = config.DB_PATH
    if db.exists():
        bak = db.with_suffix(f".db.bak-{datetime.date.today():%Y%m%d}")
        shutil.copy2(db, bak)
        print(f"[backup] {db} -> {bak}")
    Memory()  # _init_schema joue les CREATE ... IF NOT EXISTS (facets inclus)
    print("[ok] schéma facets/episode_facets présent (additif, non destructif).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `salience._tokens(s) -> set[str]` (`salience.py:14`) pour la tokenisation cohérente avec le rappel.
- Produces :
  - `facets_extract.slugify(term: str) -> str`
  - `facets_extract.TECH_ENTITY_RE: re.Pattern`
  - `facets_extract.extract_facets(content: str) -> list[tuple[str, float]]` — liste `(slug, weight)` dédupliquée en interne, triée poids desc, cap `config.FACET_MAX_PER_EPISODE`.

**Contraintes (rappel)** : **offline pur** (regex + stopwords en dur, zéro modèle, zéro réseau) ; **déterministe** (mêmes entrées → mêmes facettes) ; heuristique assumée (constantes = ordre de grandeur, spec §11).

```python
from facets_extract import extract_facets, slugify


def test_slugify_normalises():

    assert slugify("Orbit Controls") == "orbit-controls"
    assert slugify("  bbox ") == "bbox"

        "Bug de recentrage caméra dans Three.js : le centre d'orbite ignore la bbox.")}
    assert "three.js" in slugs   # entité technique (point interne)
    assert "bbox" in slugs       # mot-clé saillant

    slugs = [s for s, _w in facets]
    assert slugs.count("stripe") == 1                  # dédup interne
    assert slugs.index("stripe") < slugs.index("checkout")  # + fréquent = poids ↑ = d'abord


def test_cap_max_per_episode():

    assert len(extract_facets(long)) <= 8              # config.FACET_MAX_PER_EPISODE


def test_weights_in_unit_range():
```

```python
# --- Facettes (Phase 3.5 — précision « au pixel près », feature-based) ---
FACET_MAX_PER_EPISODE = 8    # cap de facettes tagguées par souvenir (anti-bruit)
FACET_MIN_TOKEN_LEN   = 3    # sous ce seuil, un mot-clé n'est pas une facette
```
Créer `facets_extract.py` :
```python
"""Extraction de FACETTES candidates (Phase 3.5) — 100% offline, déterministe.

Une facette = un trait saillant du souvenir. Deux sources :
  1) entités techniques (regex) : three.js, e2e, k-means, module.py, camelCase…
  2) mots-clés saillants : tokens fréquents, hors stopwords FR/EN, len >= seuil.
La précision « au pixel près » vient de l'INTERSECTION de facettes au rappel,
pas d'un arbre profond (cf. spec §2.6).
"""
import re
from collections import Counter

import config
import salience  # _tokens : tokenisation cohérente avec le rappel

# Stopwords FR + EN minimalistes (offline, pas de dépendance).
_STOP = {
    # FR
    "le", "la", "les", "un", "une", "des", "de", "du", "et", "ou", "que", "qui",
    "quoi", "dont", "avec", "sans", "pour", "par", "dans", "sur", "sous", "est",
    "sont", "ete", "etre", "cette", "ces", "ceux", "celle", "son", "sa", "ses",
    "mon", "ma", "mes", "ton", "ta", "tes", "nos", "vos", "leur", "leurs", "au",
    "aux", "en", "il", "elle", "ils", "elles", "on", "nous", "vous", "je", "tu",
    "ne", "pas", "plus", "moins", "tres", "bien", "mais", "donc", "car", "alors",
    "comme", "fait", "faire", "avoir", "ceci", "cela", "ici", "la",
    # EN
    "the", "a", "an", "of", "and", "or", "to", "in", "on", "for", "with", "is",
    "are", "was", "were", "be", "been", "this", "that", "these", "those", "it",
    "its", "as", "at", "by", "from", "not", "but", "so", "do", "does", "did",
}

# Entités techniques : garde les tokens à ponctuation interne (three.js, k-means,
# ci/cd, module.py) et le CamelCase — que salience._tokens (\w+) casserait.
TECH_ENTITY_RE = re.compile(
    r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:[._/\-][A-Za-z0-9]+)+"   # three.js, ci/cd, k-means, x.py
    r"|[a-z]+(?:[A-Z][a-z0-9]+)+"                        # orbitControls, camelCase
)


def slugify(term):
    """Slug canonique d'une facette : minuscule, espaces -> '-', ponctuation interne gardée."""
    t = (term or "").strip().lower()
    t = re.sub(r"\s+", "-", t)
    t = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9._/\-]", "", t)
    return t.strip("-")


def extract_facets(content):
    """Retourne [(slug, weight)] dédupliqué, poids desc, borné à FACET_MAX_PER_EPISODE.

    weight = fréquence normalisée (0<w<=1), entités techniques bonifiées x1.5 (cap 1.0).
    """
    content = content or ""
    counts = Counter()

    # 1) entités techniques (fort signal, bonus)
    for m in TECH_ENTITY_RE.findall(content):
        s = slugify(m)
        if len(s) >= config.FACET_MIN_TOKEN_LEN:
            counts[s] += 1.5

    # 2) mots-clés saillants (tokens \w+ cohérents avec le rappel)
    for tok in salience._tokens(content):
        if tok in _STOP or len(tok) < config.FACET_MIN_TOKEN_LEN or tok.isdigit():
            continue
        s = slugify(tok)
        if s and s not in counts:      # ne pas re-compter une entité déjà captée
            counts[s] += 1.0
        elif s:
            counts[s] += 1.0

    if not counts:
        return []
    top = counts.most_common(config.FACET_MAX_PER_EPISODE)
    maxc = top[0][1]
    return [(s, round(min(1.0, c / maxc), 3)) for s, c in top]
```

- [ ] **(3) Implémentation minimale.** Dans `memory.py`, insérer après `count()` (avant `retrieve`) :
```python
    # --- facettes (Phase 3.5 — précision « au pixel près ») ---
    def upsert_facet(self, slug):
        """Retourne l'id de la facette `slug`, en la créant si absente (dédup contre facets.slug)."""
        row = self.db.execute("SELECT id FROM facets WHERE slug = ?", (slug,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            return row[0]
        cur = self.db.execute("INSERT INTO facets (slug) VALUES (?)", (slug,))
        self.db.commit()
        return cur.lastrowid

    def tag_facets(self, episode_id, facets):
        """Lie une liste [(slug, weight)] à un épisode. Idempotent (INSERT OR IGNORE)."""
        for slug, weight in facets:
            fid = self.upsert_facet(slug)
            self.db.execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO episode_facets (episode_id, facet_id, weight) "
                "VALUES (?,?,?)",
                (episode_id, fid, float(weight)),
            )
        self.db.commit()
        return self.db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episode_facets WHERE episode_id = ?", (episode_id,)
        ).fetchone()[0]

    def facets_of(self, episode_id):
        """[(slug, weight)] des facettes d'un épisode."""
        return self.db.execute(
            "SELECT f.slug, ef.weight FROM episode_facets ef "
            "JOIN facets f ON f.id = ef.facet_id WHERE ef.episode_id = ? "
            "ORDER BY ef.weight DESC",
            (episode_id,),
        ).fetchall()
```

### Task 6 — salience.facet_overlap : Jaccard pondéré entre facettes requête et souvenir

**Files:**
- Modify `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/salience.py` — nouvelle fonction après `recency` (`salience.py:35-37`).
- Create `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_facets_recall.py` (Test — partie overlap).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : rien d'externe (fonction pure).
- Produces : `salience.facet_overlap(query_facets, row_facets) -> float` — `query_facets` = `set[str]` **ou** `dict[str,float]` ; `row_facets` = `dict[str,float]` ; retourne un **Jaccard pondéré** dans `[0,1]`. C'est *lui* qui porte le « au pixel près » (spec §5).

**Contraintes (rappel)** : **s'appuyer sur `salience.py`** (le scoring vit ici) ; pur/offline/déterministe ; robuste (entrées vides → 0.0).

Steps :
- [ ] **(1) Écrire le test qui échoue.** `tests/test_facets_recall.py` :
```python
import pytest

from salience import facet_overlap


def test_empty_returns_zero():
    assert facet_overlap(set(), {}) == 0.0
    assert facet_overlap({"a"}, {}) == 0.0
    assert facet_overlap(set(), {"a": 1.0}) == 0.0


def test_identical_sets_score_one():
    r = {"three.js": 1.0, "bbox": 1.0}
    assert facet_overlap({"three.js", "bbox"}, r) == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_partial_overlap_between_0_and_1():
    r = {"three.js": 1.0, "bbox": 1.0, "orbit": 1.0}
    s = facet_overlap({"three.js"}, r)
    assert 0.0 < s < 1.0


def test_weighted_jaccard_rewards_heavy_shared_facets():
    q = {"camera": 1.0, "misc": 1.0}
    heavy = {"camera": 1.0, "other": 0.1}
    light = {"camera": 0.1, "other": 1.0}
    assert facet_overlap(q, heavy) > facet_overlap(q, light)


def test_accepts_set_query():
    assert facet_overlap({"x"}, {"x": 0.5}) > 0.0
```

- [ ] **(3) Implémentation minimale.** Ajouter à la fin de `salience.py` :
```python
def facet_overlap(query_facets, row_facets):
    """Jaccard PONDÉRÉ entre les facettes de la requête et celles d'un souvenir (0..1).

    query_facets : set[str] (poids implicite 1.0) ou dict[str,float].
    row_facets    : dict[str,float].
    C'est le terme qui porte le « au pixel près » (intersection de traits, spec §5).
    """
    if not query_facets or not row_facets:
        return 0.0
    if not isinstance(query_facets, dict):
        query_facets = {s: 1.0 for s in query_facets}
    keys = set(query_facets) | set(row_facets)
    inter = 0.0
    union = 0.0
    for k in keys:
        a = float(query_facets.get(k, 0.0))
        b = float(row_facets.get(k, 0.0))
        inter += min(a, b)
        union += max(a, b)
    return inter / union if union > 0.0 else 0.0
```

```python
def test_facet_overlap_boosts_pixel_match(mem):
```

```python
    def facets_bulk(self):
        """{episode_id: [(slug, weight)]} pour tous les épisodes (1 scan, pour le rappel)."""
        rows = self.db.execute(
            "SELECT ef.episode_id, f.slug, ef.weight FROM episode_facets ef "
            "JOIN facets f ON f.id = ef.facet_id"
        ).fetchall()
        out = {}
        for ep_id, slug, w in rows:
            out.setdefault(ep_id, []).append((slug, w))
        return out
```
Toujours dans `memory.py`, remplacer le `retrieve` par (SELECT ajoute `id`; calcul du terme facettes) :
```python
    def retrieve(self, query_text, query_emb, now=None, top_k=config.TOP_K, kinds=None):
        """Récupération HYBRIDE sparse : sémantique + lexical + saillance + FACETTES.

        kinds : si fourni (ex. ['reasoning']), ne garde que ces types.
        """
        now = now or time.time()
        rows = self.db.execute(
            "SELECT id, ts, role, content, importance, embedding FROM episodes"
        ).fetchall()
        import facets_extract
        q_facets = dict(facets_extract.extract_facets(query_text))  # {slug: weight}
        all_facets = self.facets_bulk()
        scored = []
        for ep_id, ts, role, content, importance, emb_json in rows:
            if kinds and role not in kinds:
                continue
            emb = json.loads(emb_json) if emb_json else None
            rel = (
                salience.cosine(query_emb, emb)
                if (query_emb and emb and len(emb) == len(query_emb))
                else 0.0
            )
            lex = salience.lexical_overlap(query_text, content)
            rec = salience.recency(ts, now)
            row_facets = dict(all_facets.get(ep_id, []))
            fac = salience.facet_overlap(q_facets, row_facets) if q_facets else 0.0
            s = (
                config.W_RELEVANCE * rel
                + config.W_LEXICAL * lex
                + config.W_RECENCY * rec
                + config.W_IMPORTANCE * float(importance)
                + config.W_FACET * fac
            )
            scored.append(
                (
                    s,
                    ts,
                    role,
                    content,
                    {
                        "rel": round(rel, 3), "lex": round(lex, 3),
                        "rec": round(rec, 3), "fac": round(fac, 3),
                        "fac_slugs": list(row_facets.keys()),
                        "content": content,
                    },
                )
            )
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        return scored[:top_k]
```
(Le 5e élément — dict de debug — gagne `fac`, `fac_slugs`, `content`. Les consommateurs existants lisent `rel/lex/rec` par clé → **rétro-compatible**.)

### Task 8 — Backfill migration : extraction facettes sur les épisodes existants (offline, idempotent)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `facets_extract.extract_facets`, `Memory.tag_facets`, table `episodes`.
- Produces : `Memory.backfill_facets(self, extract_fn=None) -> int` — parcourt tous les épisodes **sans facettes**, extrait+tague, retourne le nb d'épisodes traités. **Idempotent** (rejouable, INSERT OR IGNORE + saut des déjà-taggés).

**Contraintes (rappel)** : **migration = extraction auto AUSSI** (périmètre : « à l'écriture ET à la migration ») ; **offline** (extraction locale) ; **idempotent/réversible** (backup fait par le script en amont, Task 2) ; ne touche jamais `data/memory.db` en test (`tmp_path`).

```python
def test_backfill_tags_legacy_episodes(mem):
    # simule des épisodes "legacy" écrits SANS facettes
    mem.add("user", "modelisation 3d camera orbit three.js", extract_facets=False)
    mem.add("assistant", "stripe pricing checkout webhook", extract_facets=False)

               for r in mem.db.execute("SELECT id FROM episodes").fetchall())

    n = mem.backfill_facets()

    ids = [r[0] for r in mem.db.execute("SELECT id FROM episodes ORDER BY id").fetchall()]
    assert {s for s, _w in mem.facets_of(ids[0])} >= {"three.js"}
    assert {s for s, _w in mem.facets_of(ids[1])} >= {"stripe"}


def test_backfill_idempotent(mem):

    first = mem.backfill_facets()
    second = mem.backfill_facets()      # tout est déjà taggé
    assert first == 1
    assert second == 0                  # rien à re-traiter
    ep_id = mem.db.execute("SELECT id FROM episodes LIMIT 1").fetchone()[0]
    assert len(mem.facets_of(ep_id)) == len({s for s, _ in mem.facets_of(ep_id)})  # pas de doublon


def test_backfill_skips_empty_content(mem):

    ep_id = mem.db.execute("SELECT id FROM episodes LIMIT 1").fetchone()[0]
    assert mem.facets_of(ep_id) == []
```

```python
    def backfill_facets(self, extract_fn=None):
        """Extrait+tague les facettes des épisodes NON encore taggés (migration, idempotent).

        extract_fn(content)->[(slug,weight)] ; défaut = facets_extract.extract_facets.
        Retourne le nombre d'épisodes traités.
        """
        if extract_fn is None:
            import facets_extract
            extract_fn = facets_extract.extract_facets
        already = {r[0] for r in self.db.execute(
            "SELECT DISTINCT episode_id FROM episode_facets").fetchall()}
        rows = self.db.execute("SELECT id, content FROM episodes").fetchall()
        n = 0
        for ep_id, content in rows:
            if ep_id in already:
                continue
            self.tag_facets(ep_id, extract_fn(content))
            n += 1
        return n
```

### Task 9 — Auto-split k-means offline (numpy pur) : profondeur max 6, SPLIT_MAX=12

**Files:**

- Create `/Users/admin/Desktop/anamnese/tests/test_auto_split.py` (Test).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes : `numpy` (déjà installé, 2.5.0). **Pas** de sklearn (absent → k-means maison, offline).
- Produces :
  - `config.SPLIT_MAX: int = 12`, `config.RUBRIC_MAX_DEPTH: int = 6`.
  - `auto_split.kmeans_offline(vectors: list[list[float]], k: int, iters: int = 25, seed: int = 0) -> list[int]` — labels (Lloyd, init déterministe k-means++ à seed fixé).
  - `auto_split.should_split(count: int, depth: int) -> bool` — `count > config.SPLIT_MAX and depth < config.RUBRIC_MAX_DEPTH`.

**Contraintes (rappel)** : **100% offline/gratuit** (numpy pur, aucune dépendance nouvelle) ; **déterministe** (seed) ; garde-fous d'arbre : **profondeur max 6** (au-delà, on NE split plus — on s'appuie sur les facettes combinatoires, spec §2.6), `SPLIT_MAX=12` (split tôt = fin). Capacité isolable → **déposée `modules/proposed/`** possible (promotion humaine, cf. `modules/registry.py`).

> **Note isolation** : la spec demande de faire passer les capacités isolables par `modules/proposed/`. `auto_split` étant un utilitaire pur consommé plus tard par le routage d'arbre (Phase 1/2), on peut le créer directement à `auto_split.py` (racine, importable par les tests) OU `modules/proposed/auto_split.py`. Le plan met le code à `auto_split.py` (racine) pour l'import de test simple ; ajouter une ligne de dépôt `cp auto_split.py modules/proposed/` au commit si l'on veut le marquer « proposé, non promu ».
